enterprise software isolates human resource management from financial accounting, forcing organizations to rely on manual spreadsheets, paper memos, and error-prone double-entry bookkeeping. This platform addresses this critical gap by delivering:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cstrong\u003eThe Pivotal HR-to-Finance Bridge:\u003c/strong\u003e Approving a class=\"js-inline-math\" style=\"display: inline-block\" data-run-id=\"4308fc2fd27292bfdd17dc5067327a81\"\u003e$\\sum \\text{Debit} == \\sum \\text{Credit}$\u003c/math-renderer\u003e) without human transcription.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cstrong\u003ePredictive AI Intelligence:\u003c/strong\u003e Integrates machine learning directly into operations — forecasting 30/60/90-day cash flow liquidity, parsing scanned vendor invoices via computer vision OCR, and guarding timesheets with statistical anomaly detection.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cstrong\u003eFull Egyptian Regulatory Compliance:\u003c/strong\u003e Natively implements the \u003cstrong\u003e14% Egyptian VAT\u003c/strong\u003e, electronic invoicing QR codes, withholding taxes, and labor law leave and <div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">System Analysis &amp; Design Specification (SAD / SDD)</h3><a id="user-content-system-analysis--design-specification-sad--sdd" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: System Analysis &amp; Design Specification (SAD / SDD)" href="#system-analysis--design-specification-sad--sdd"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<p dir="auto"><strong>A state-of-the-art, academic and enterprise-grade System Analysis and Design specification for a comprehensive Accounting &amp; HR ERP system augmented by predictive machine learning.</strong></p>
<p dir="auto"><em>Designed with Egyptian statutory tax compliance (14% VAT), Egyptian Labor Law (Law No. 12 of 2003), Egyptian banking integration (CIB, NBE, Banque Misr), and predictive artificial intelligence.</em></p>

<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">📖 Executive Summary &amp; Repository Purpose</h2><a id="user-content--executive-summary--repository-purpose" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 📖 Executive Summary &amp; Repository Purpose" href="#-executive-summary--repository-purpose"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">This repository houses the complete, authoritative <strong>System Analysis and Design (SAD / SDD)</strong> documentation and architectural blueprints for <strong>Indigo ERP with AI</strong>.</p>
<p dir="auto">Traditional enterprise software isolates human resource management from financial accounting, forcing organizations to rely on manual spreadsheets, paper memos, and error-prone double-entry bookkeeping. This platform addresses this critical gap by delivering:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>
<strong>The Pivotal HR-to-Finance Bridge:</strong> Approving a monthly payroll cycle instantly calculates progressive Egyptian income taxes and social insurance, generating atomic, balanced General Ledger journal vouchers (<math-renderer class="js-inline-math" style="display: inline-block" data-run-id="4308fc2fd27292bfdd17dc5067327a81">$\sum \text{Debit} == \sum \text{Credit}$</math-renderer>) without human transcription.</li>
<li>
<strong>Predictive AI Intelligence:</strong> Integrates machine learning directly into operations — forecasting 30/60/90-day cash flow liquidity, parsing scanned vendor invoices via computer vision OCR, and guarding timesheets with statistical anomaly detection.</li>
<li>
<strong>Full Egyptian Regulatory Compliance:</strong> Natively implements the <strong>14% Egyptian VAT</strong>, electronic invoicing QR codes, withholding taxes, and labor law leave and overtime rules.</li>
</ol>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🏗️ System Architecture Overview</h2><a id="user-content-️-system-architecture-overview" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🏗️ System Architecture Overview" href="#️-system-architecture-overview"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
